Also load when the user says 为什么这么写 / 这段代码的来历 / 为什么选这个方案 / 设计动因 / 历史原因 / 这个阈值怎么来的. Not for runtime behavior (how) or for finding what a change breaks (blast-radius). ([Loner1024/skills](https://github.com/Loner1024/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/why))
- **why**: Find why code is the way it is. Use for "why does X work this way", "why did we pick Y", regressions, postmortems, or before changing unexplained code. ([luizhcrocha/skills](https://github.com/luizhcrocha/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/why))
- **why**: Use for 'why does X work this way', 'why we picked Y', design rationale, regressions, postmortems, or data-backed thresholds. Discovers available MCP servers and queries each evidence category (source control, issue tracker, long-form docs, real-time chat, infrastructure observability, error tracking, product analytics warehouse) in parallel, then returns a cited read on decisions and tradeoffs. Use how for runtime behavior. ([Lyahx/hstack](https://github.com/Lyahx/hstack/tree/HEAD/skills/why))
- **why**: Find out why code is the way it is (design rationale, past decisions, regressions, where a threshold came from) by searching git, PRs and every connected ticket, doc, chat and observability tool in parallel, and return a cited, confidence-tiered answer. - **why**: Investigate why code, a design or a decision is the way it is, and return a cited answer that separates what was found from what is inferred and what is unknown. Reads the decision records, git history, pull request bodies and reviews, and issues, and any other evidence source the session offers. Use for 'why does X work this way', 'why we picked Y', design rationale, regressions, postmortems, or data-backed thresholds, and before changing code whose reason is not visible. For how the code behaves at runtime, use discover:how. ([lyngon/devkit](https://github.com/lyngon/devkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/discover/skills/why))

- **why-startups-fail**: 当用户递交一份创业规划、描述正在进行的创业想法与经营打算，或询问"我这项目有什么风险/为什么起不来/该不该继续"时使用： 先按《Why Startups Fail》的六种失败模式（好点子坏伙伴 / 抢跑 / 假阳性 / 速度陷阱 / 招人难 / 级联奇迹）对号入座， 给出清晰的风险提醒（含早期预警信号与对应证据），必要时做失败解剖（定义/四类风险/厄运与失误归因）、 钻石方框对齐体检、资源死结破解、止损决策；最后必须给一份建设性的"阳光路线图"（亮点 + 最小下一步 + 里程碑）。 来自 Tom Eisenmann《Why Startups Fail》(2021)。不适用：纯书摘/读后感、扮演作者本人、与创业无关的决策、纯法律财务操作咨询。 另含**创业风险诊断室**：当用户说"创业风险诊断 / 帮我看看我这项目 / 诊断一下我的创业计划 / 压力测试 / 模拟投资人"时，本技能会先追问关键事实，再输出「分诊表 + 风险提醒 + 阳光路线图」， 并可选扮演刁钻投资人做对抗式压力测试（详见 references/diagnostic-dialogue.md）。 ([Atange2023/why-startups-fail-skill](https://github.com/Atange2023/why-startups-fail-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
